Pseudo:
def project(x,n):
  target=n/2
  lo=np.min(x)-1, hi=np.max(x)+1
  for 80: mid=(lo+hi)/2; s=np.clip(x-mid,0,1).sum(); if s>target: lo=mid else hi=mid
  y=clip(x-mid,0,1); adjust residual diff=target-y.sum(); distribute to elements with 0<y<1 (or if none, to extremes with room) in small amounts, clip. Because projection should exact. We can add diff to all interior if any: y=clip(y+diff/num_interior,0,1), repeat few. If no interior, if all 0/1 but sum not target impossible? target half and bounds, can adjust one: if diff>0 increase elements <1; else decrease >0. Loop. Return.
This is closer than iterative.

Let's evaluate smooth L-BFGS-B approach more concretely.

Function:
def smooth_object(x, beta, lam, n):
   c = np.correlate(x, 1-x, mode='full')
   m = c.max()
   # stable logsum: z=beta*(c-m); q=exp(z)/sum
   logsum = m + (1/beta)*log(sum(exp(z)))
   base= (2/n)*logsum
   s=x.sum()-n/2
   return base + lam*s*s
Gradient:
   z= beta*(c-m); expz; q=expz/expz.sum()
   # q length 2n-1
   grad_c = fft_corr(1-x, q) - fft_corr(x, qneg) * dx
   grad = grad_c + 2*lam*s*np.ones(n)
where qneg as described. Need ensure FFT correlation returns correct length N and slice.

In code final, we can choose loop for gradient if N small (n<=1000) maybe safer than FFT. n=700 loop 1400 vector operations per gradient; maybe okay. For L-BFGS-B 200 eval -> 280k vector ops, maybe 5-10s. Let's use direct loop to avoid FFT bugs. But could be slow if many. We can implement both: for n<=1500, direct vectorization over shifts optimized with for loop over s. 1400 iterations per grad; each creates slice views and adds weighted. 1400 Python iterations maybe 0.005? 200 =1s? Direct gradient function:
def grad_smooth(x,q,beta? no beta), lam=0:
   n=len(x); b=1-x; F=zeros; G=zeros
   # We need q array shifts from -(n-1) to n-1? q_full from correlate order: index0 shift=-(n-1), idx n-1 shift=0, idx n shift=1.
   for idx in range(2*n-1):
      qv=q[idx]
      if qv ==0: continue
      s = idx - (n-1)
      if s >= 0:
        rem = n - s
        F[:rem] += qv * b[s:]
        G[s:] += qv * x[:rem]
      else:
        t = -s
        rem = n - t
        F[t:] += qv * b[:rem]
        G[:rem] += qv * x[t:]
   grad = (F - G)*(2/n)
   if lam: grad += 2*lam*(x.sum()-n/2)
Check s=0 included: F +=q b all, G +=q x all -> q(1-2x). Good.

Precompute CD:
def precompute_cd(n):
  pre=[]
  for p in range(n):
    R=max(p,n-1-p)
    mlo=n-1-R; mhi=n-1+R
    k = np.arange(-R,R+1)
    vf = (p - k >=0) & (p - k < n)
    vs = (p + k >=0) & (p + k < n)
    posf = np.nonzero(vf)[0]
    if posf.size: idxf = (p - k[vf]).astype(np.int64)
    else empty
    poss = np.nonzero(vs)[0]
    if poss.size: idxs = (p + k[vs]).astype(np.int64)
    pre.append((mlo,mhi,posf,idxf,poss,idxs,R,diag_pos=R))

`_eval_points`: 
```
def eval_cd_points(c_aff, a, diag, unaff, ds):
    pts = np.asarray(ds)
    vals = c_aff[:, None] + a[:, None] * pts[None, :]
    vals[diag, :] -= pts[None, :] * pts[None, :]
    f = vals.max(axis=0)
    if unaff > 0:
        f = np.maximum(f, unaff)
    return f
```
But `vals[diag,:] -=` modifies vals; okay. If pts scalar? Make array. For golden, use 2 pts.

Implementation:
```
def smooth_value(x,n,beta,lam):
    c=np.correlate(x,1-x,'full')
    m=c.max()
    z=beta*(c-m)
    # Avoid exp overflow: subtract m -> <=0, fine. But if beta large and c-m large positive? m max so <=0.
    ez=np.exp(z)
    logsum=np.log(ez.sum())
    val=(2/n)*(m + logsum/beta)
    s=x.sum()-n/2
    if lam: val += lam*s*s
    return val
```
Could logsum underflow if all ez 0? m at max gives exp(0)=1, sum>=1, so no.
Gradient:
```
def smooth_grad(x,n,beta,lam):
    c=...
    m=c.max(); z=beta*(c-m); ez=exp(z); q=ez/ez.sum()
    b=1-x
    F=zeros(n); G=zeros(n)
    q0=q[n-1]; F += q0*b; G += q0*x
    # loop t
    for t in range(1,n):
       qp=q[n-1-t]
       if qp !=0:
          rem=n-t; F[:rem]+=qp*b[t:]; G[t:]+=qp*x[:rem]
       qm=q[n-1+t]
       if qm !=0:
          rem=n-t; F[t:]+=qm*b[:rem]; G[:rem]+=qm*x[t:]
    grad=(F-G)*(2/n)
    if lam: grad += 2*lam*(x.sum()-n/2)
    return grad
```
Potential q sum 1. For high beta, q mostly maxes. If m ties, q spread. Good.

We can speed by precomputing for t slices? Not needed. The loop 700 per gradient; if L-BFGS-B 200 evaluations -> 140k loops, okay.

Potential numerical at beta high: z for entries far below m underflow to -inf, exp 0. fine. But if beta=1e6, z may be -1e5, exp 0. fine. `np.exp` of array with large negative okay (underflow warning? maybe). Could suppress? Not necessary, but can avoid by clipping z > -700? Since only need relative; `z=np.maximum(beta*(c-m), -745)`. But then entries far below get small nonzero, q not exactly one-hot; okay. We can do `z=beta*(c-m); z[z < -700]= -700` to avoid underflow? Underflow is fine (returns 0). But if all nonmax underflow, ez max=1. Good.

```
def _lb_val(x, n, beta, lam): return smooth_value(...)
def _lb_jac(x, n, beta, lam): return smooth_grad(...)

```

- p0(d)=c0 + a0 d - d^2 ≤ M => d^2 - a0 d + (M - c0) ≥ 0? Since max ≤M. Equivalent d outside interval where p0>M.
- lines: c_m + a_m d ≤ M => if a_m>0, d ≤ (M-c_m)/a_m; if a_m<0, d ≥ (M-c_m)/a_m; if a_m=0, need c_m≤M.

Let's define:
```
def eval_cd_points(c_aff,a,diag,unaff,ds):
    ds=np.asarray(ds,dtype=float)
    if ds.size==1:
       ds=ds.reshape(2)? no.
    vals = c_aff[:,None] + a[:,None]*ds[None,:]
    vals[diag,:] -= ds*ds
    f=vals.max(axis=0)
    if unaff>0:
       np.maximum(f, unaff, out=f)
    return f
```

Implement `project_feasible(x,n)` robust:
```
def project_feasible(x,n):
  target=n/2.0
  x=np.asarray(x,dtype=float)
  # initial clip
  x=np.clip(x,0,1)
  # binary search lambda
  lo=x.min()-1.0; hi=x.max()+1.0
  for _ in range(80):
    mid=0.5*(lo+hi)
    s=np.clip(x-mid,0,1).sum()
    if s>target: lo=mid
    else: hi=mid
  y=np.clip(x-0.5*(lo+hi),0,1)
  # adjust residual
  for _ in range(10):
    diff=target-y.sum()
    if abs(diff)<1e-13: break
    interior=(y>1e-12)&(y<1-1e-12)
    if interior.any():
       y=clip(y + diff/sum(interior),0,1)
    else:
       # add to smallest/largest
       if diff>0:
          order=np.argsort(1-y)
          k=int(np.ceil(diff))
          y[order[:k]] = np.minimum(y[order[:k]]+diff? careful if diff>1 max count)
       else...
  return y
```
But adjusting with diff/num_interior may push some to bounds; iterate. Since y from projection sum close to target; residual tiny. 10 enough. If no interior and residual nonzero (e.g. all 0/1), sum might not target if target not integer? target n/2 integer if n even. If n odd? Problem sum n/2 exactly maybe half-integer; with binary impossible. Use fractional. Residual small.

Let's implement robust `enforce_sum(y,n)`:
```
target=n/2
for _ in range(20):
    diff=target-y.sum()
    if abs(diff)<1e-14: break
    if diff>0:
        room=1-y
        tot=room.sum()
        if tot<=0: break
        y=np.clip(y + diff*room/tot,0,1)
    else:
        tot=y.sum()
        if tot<=0: break
        y=np.clip(y + diff*y/tot,0,1)
# final correction with one variable to fix residual
res=target-y.sum()
if res>0:
    idx=np.where(y<1.0)[0]
    for i in idx:
       inc=min(res,1-y[i]); y[i]+=inc; res-=inc; if res<=1e-15:break
elif res<0:
    idx=np.where(y>0.0)[0]
    for i in idx:
       dec=min(-res,y[i]); y[i]-=dec; res+=dec; if abs(res)<=1e-15:break
```
This ensures within 1e-15. The loop is O(n*20) but n=700 okay. But doing projection many times (passes*freq) maybe 1000 times => 1.4M, fine.

Smooth value:
```
def smooth_value(x,n,beta,mu):
   c=corr; m; logsum; val=(2/n)*(m+logsum/beta)
   s=(x.sum()-n/2)*(2/n)
   return val + mu*s*s
def smooth_grad(...):
   base_grad as dx*(F-G)
   s=(x.sum()-n/2)*(2/n)
   return base_grad + 2*mu*s*dx*ones
```
Where dx=2/n. Good.

Let's refine `project_feasible`:
```
def project_feasible(x, n):
    tgt = 0.5*n
    x = np.clip(np.asarray(x, dtype=float), 0.0, 1.0)
    lo = float(np.min(x)) - 1.0
    hi = float(np.max(x)) + 1.0
    for _ in range(60):
        mid = 0.5*(lo+hi)
        s = float(np.clip(x - mid, 0.0, 1.0).sum())
        if s > tgt:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid
    y = np.clip(x - 0.5*(lo+hi), 0.0, 1.0)
    # enforce sum
    for _ in range(10):
        diff = tgt - y.sum()
        if abs(diff) < 1e-13:
            break
        if diff > 0:
            room = 1.0 - y
            tot = float(room.sum())
            if tot <= 0: break
            y = np.clip(y + diff*room/tot, 0.0, 1.0)
        else:
            tot = float(y.sum())
            if tot <= 0: break
            y = np.clip(y + diff*y/tot, 0.0, 1.0)
    # final tiny correction
    for _ in range(50):
        res = tgt - y.sum()
        if abs(res) <= 1e-14: break
        if res > 0:
            idx = np.where(y < 1.0)[0]
            if idx.size == 0: break
            # add to elements with most room? Use proportional already maybe enough; do direct first
            inc = min(res, 1.0 - y[idx[0]])
            y[idx[0]] += inc
            res -= inc
        else:
            idx = np.where(y > 0.0)[0]
            if idx.size == 0: break
            dec = min(-res, y[idx[0]])
            y[idx[0]] -= dec
            res += dec
    return y
```
The final loop adds to same idx[0] repeatedly if room large; okay. But if res > room, inc=room, res positive, next loop y[idx[0]]=1, idx excludes, okay. This while up to 50; n small. But adding to one variable may create large local change; residual likely tiny, so no. Proportional adjusts sum within 1e-13. Good.
Potential issue: binary search direction: We want sum(clip(x-λ,0,1)) = target. As λ increases, sum decreases. If s > target, need larger λ, so lo=mid. Good.

`precompute_cd`:
```
def precompute_cd(n):
    pre=[]
    empty=np.empty(0,dtype=np.int64)
    for p in range(n):
        R = max(p, n-1-p)
        mlo = n-1-R
        mhi = n-1+R
        k = np.arange(-R, R+1, dtype=np.int64)
        vf = (p - k >= 0) & (p - k < n)
        vs = (p + k >= 0) & (p + k < n)
        pf = np.nonzero(vf)[0]
        if pf.size:
            if_= (p - k[vf]).astype(np.int64)
        else: if_=empty
        ps = np.nonzero(vs)[0]
        if ps.size:
            id_= (p + k[vs]).astype(np.int64)
        else: id_=empty
        pre.append((mlo,mhi,pf,if_,ps,id_,R))
    return pre
```
Variable names avoid if. Use `posf, idxf`.

Memory: `k` array per p; large total but freed. Pre storing pos/idx. Could use `np.empty(0,int)` for empty; okay.

def eval_cd_points(c_aff, a, diag, unaff, ds):

    vals = c_aff[:, None] + a[:, None] * ds[None, :]
    vals[diag, :] -= ds[:, 0]? Wait ds 1D. Use vals[diag, :] -= ds * ds
    f = vals.max(axis=0)
    if unaff > f.max()? Actually need max with unaff per point: `if unaff > 0: f = np.maximum(f, unaff)`.
    return f
```

`coord_descent` detailed:
```
def coord_descent(h, n, deadline, max_passes=100, grid_points=32, gold_iters=18, proj_every=50, seed=0):
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    best_c = compute_c5(h,n)
    if max_passes <=0: return h,best_c
    pre = precompute_cd(n)
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    no_imp=0
    for pno in range(max_passes):
        if time.time() > deadline: break
        c = np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, mode='full')
        cur_cmax = float(c.max())
        pass_improve = False
        order = rng.permutation(n)
        for cnt, p in enumerate(order):
            if time.time() > deadline: break
            if cnt and cnt % proj_every == 0:
                h = project_feasible(h,n)
                c = np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full')
            h0 = h[p]
            if h0 <= 0.0:
                lo = 0.0; hi=1.0
            elif h0 >= 1.0:
                lo = -1.0; hi=0.0
            else:
                lo = -h0; hi=1.0-h0
            if hi - lo < 1e-13: continue
            mlo,mhi,posf,idxf,poss,idxs,R = pre[p]
            L = 2*R+1
            a = np.zeros(L, dtype=float)
            if posf.size:
                a[posf] += 1.0 - h[idxf]
            if poss.size:
                a[poss] -= h[idxs]
            c_aff = c[mlo:mhi+1]
            if mlo > 0:
                u1 = float(c[:mlo].max())
            else: u1=0.0
            if mhi + 1 < 2*n-1:
                u2 = float(c[mhi+1:].max())
            else: u2=0.0
            unaff = u1 if u1>u2 else u2
            f0_arr = eval_cd_points(c_aff, a, R, unaff, [0.0])
            f0 = float(f0_arr[0])
            # current max should be f0? f0 uses c maybe current overall max? It includes unaff and affected, yes. f0 == cmax? All affected include all p may miss some c? The c_aff+unaff covers all, so f0 = cmax. But due stale? c updated, yes.
            ds = np.linspace(lo, hi, grid_points)
            fgrid = eval_cd_points(c_aff,a,R,unaff,ds)
            i = int(np.argmin(fgrid))
            best_d = float(ds[i]); best_f = float(fgrid[i])
            if best_f < f0 - 1e-10:
                # refine
                if i == 0:
                    lo_r = ds[0]; hi_r = min(hi, ds[1] if grid_points>1 else hi)
                elif i == grid_points-1:
                    lo_r = ds[-2]; hi_r = ds[-1]
                else:
                    lo_r = ds[i-1]; hi_r = ds[i+1]
                # golden using eval_cd_points with 2 points
                gr = 0.6180339887498949
                d1 = hi_r - gr*(hi_r-lo_r)
                d2 = lo_r + gr*(hi_r-lo_r)
                f1 = float(eval_cd_points(c_aff,a,R,unaff,[d1])[0])
                f2 = float(eval_cd_points(c_aff,a,R,unaff,[d2])[0])
                for _ in range(gold_iters):
                    if hi_r - lo_r < 1e-12: break
                    if f1 < f2:
                        hi_r = d2; d2 = d1; f2 = f1
                        d1 = hi_r - gr*(hi_r-lo_r)
                        f1 = float(eval_cd_points(c_aff,a,R,unaff,[d1])[0])
                    else:
                        lo_r = d1; d1 = d2; f1 = f2
                        d2 = lo_r + gr*(hi_r-lo_r)
                        f2 = float(eval_cd_points(c_aff,a,R,unaff,[d2])[0])
                dcand = (lo_r+hi_r)/2
                fdcand = float(eval_cd_points(c_aff,a,R,unaff,[dcand])[0])
                if fdcand < best_f:
                    best_f=fdcand; best_d=dcand
                elif min(f1,f2) < best_f:
                    # maybe endpoints? Use min of current d1,d2? But golden updated f1/f2; choose accordingly
                    if f1 <= f2:
                       best_f=f1; best_d=d1
                    else: best_f=f2; best_d=d2
                if best_f < f0 - 1e-10:
                    h[p] = h0 + best_d
                    # update c incrementally
                    delta = a * best_d
                    delta[R] -= best_d*best_d
                    c[mlo:mhi+1] += delta
                    pass_improve = True
        if cnt+1 < n: break
        h = project_feasible(h,n)
        c_end = np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full')
        end_c = float(c_end.max())
        if end_c < best_c - 1e-12:
            best_c = end_c
            best_h = h.copy()
            no_imp=0
        else:
            # keep h (feishible) even if no improvement
            h = h
            no_imp += 1
            if no_imp >= 3:
                break
    return h, best_c? Wait if h improved but c_end not < best_c? best_c tracked. Return h,best_c but h may correspond to best_c? If no_imp break, h is current feasible, c maybe best_c (updated if improved). If best_c from earlier but h later didn't improve (maybe slightly worse after projection?), best_h not stored. We should store best_h whenever best_c improves, and at final if current c lower use. But if current h worse than best_h, we should return best_h. Coordinate descent should not worsen much, but projection no_imp could increase. To be safe, maintain `best_h` when `end_c < best_c`. At end, if compute current c < best_c update; else return best_h,best_c.
```

`lbfgs_run`:
```
def lbfgs_run(x0,n,beta,mu,maxiter):
   try:
      bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
      res=minimize(_lbfun, x0, args=(n,beta,mu), jac=_lbjac, method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds, options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-12,'gtol':1e-6,'maxls':20})
      if res.x is None: return x0
      x=project_feasible(res.x,n)
      return x
   except Exception:
      return x0
```
We don't compute c inside; caller updates.

`lbfgs_polish`:

def lbfgs_polish(x,n,deadline, mu=200.0, betas=None):
  x=project
  best_x=x.copy(); best_c=compute_c5(x)
  if betas is None: betas=[50,200,1000,5000,20000]
  for beta in betas:
    if time.time() > deadline - 40: break
    maxiter = 80 if beta<1000 else 120
    y = lbfgs_run(x,n,beta,mu,maxiter)
    c=compute_c5(y,n)
    if c < best_c:
       best_c=c; best_x=y.copy(); x=y
    else:
       # try from best_x with small perturbation?
       pass
  return best_x,best_c
```

```
def feasible_cd(c_aff,a,diag,unaff,lo,hi,M):
    if unaff > M + 1e-12: return False, None

```
def cdfeasible(M, lo, hi, unaff, c_pos, a_pos, c_neg, a_neg, zero_c_max, c0, a0):
    if M < unaff - 1e-11: return False, 0.0
    Lb=lo; Ub=hi
    if c_pos.size:
       ub=float(np.min((M - c_pos)/a_pos))
       if ub < Ub: Ub=ub
    if c_neg.size:
       lb=float(np.max((M - c_neg)/a_neg))
       if lb > Lb: Lb=lb
    if zero_c_max > M + 1e-11: return False, 0.0
    if Lb > Ub + 1e-10: return False, 0.0
    A=a0
    disc = A*A - 4.0*(M - c0)
    if disc > 0.0:
       sqrt=np.sqrt(disc)
       r1=0.5*(A - sqrt); r2=0.5*(A + sqrt)
       # if [Lb,Ub] contained in (r1,r2) infeasible
       if Lb >= r1 - 1e-10 and Ub <= r2 + 1e-10:
           return False, 0.0
       if Ub <= r1 + 1e-10:
           d=Ub
       elif Lb >= r2 - 1e-10:
           d=Lb
       elif Lb < r1 - 1e-10:
           d=Lb
       else:
           d=Ub
    else:
       d=0.5*(Lb+Ub)
    if d < lo: d=lo
    elif d > hi: d=hi
    return True,d
```

Let's craft `smooth_grad` efficient with local variables:
```
def smooth_grad(x,n,beta,mu):
    c=np.correlate(x,1.0-x,mode='full')
    m=c.max()
    z=beta*(c-m)
    z=np.maximum(z,-745.0)
    ez=np.exp(z)
    q=ez/ez.sum()
    b=1.0-x
    F=np.zeros(n); G=np.zeros(n)
    q0=q[n-1]
    F += q0*b; G += q0*x
    for t in range(1,n):
        rem=n-t
        qp=q[n-1-t]
        if qp != 0.0:
            F[:rem] += qp*b[t:]
            G[t:] += qp*x[:rem]
        qm=q[n-1+t]
        if qm != 0.0:
            F[t:] += qm*b[:rem]
            G[:rem] += qm*x[t:]
    dx=2.0/n
    grad=(F-G)*dx
    s=(x.sum()-0.5*n)*dx
    if mu:
       grad += 2.0*mu*s*dx
    return grad
```
Wait penalty gradient: val = base + mu*(integral_error)^2, integral_error = (sum-target)*dx. derivative = 2*mu*integral_error*dx. Yes. `dx=2/n`. So add `2*mu*s*dx`, where s already integral error? I set `s=(x.sum()-target)*dx`. Then grad += 2*mu*s*dx? Let's recalc: e = (sum-target)*dx. val += mu e^2. dval/dx_i = 2 mu e * dx. So yes `2*mu*e*dx`. Good.

Smooth value:
```
def smooth_value(x,n,beta,mu):
   c=..., m; z; ez; logsum; base=dx*(m+logsum/beta)
   e=(x.sum()-0.5*n)*dx
   return base + mu*e*e
```

I think yes, func args passed to both if jac is a function. According docs, if jac is '2-step' no; but function uses same args. So `_lbjac(x,n,beta,mu)` okay.

F=M + (1/β)log S. dF = dM + (1/β)(β∑softmax dt_i - β∑softmax dM) = dM(1-∑softmax) + softmax⋅dt = softmax⋅dt since ∑softmax=1. This uses dM = ∑ w_max dt where w_max subgradient of max? `lbfgs_run`:
```
def lbfgs_run(x0,n,beta,mu,maxiter,stop_at):
    LB_STOP[0]=stop_at
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
    try:
       res=minimize(_lbfun, x0, args=(n,beta,mu), jac=_lbjac, method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds,
                    callback=lb_callback, options={'maxiter': int(maxiter), 'ftol':1e-12, 'gtol':1e-7, 'maxls':25})
       x=project_feasible(np.asarray(res.x,dtype=float), n)
       return x
    except Exception:
       return x0
```

`lbfgs_polish`: Use current best to start each; if no improvement after a beta, maybe perturb for next. Use rng? `run` has seed; pass rng? L-BFGS doesn't need.
```
def lbfgs_polish(x,n,deadline):
   x=project_feasible(x,n); best=compute_c5(x,n); best_x=x.copy()
   betas=[50.0,200.0,1000.0,5000.0,20000.0]
   rng=np.random.RandomState(7)
   for i,beta in enumerate(betas):
      if time.time() > deadline-45: break
      maxiter=60 if beta<1000 else 100
      start=best_x
      if i>1 and compute_c5(start,n) < best-1e-12:
          # maybe perturb
          start=project_feasible(best_x + 0.01*rng.normal(size=n), n)
      y=lbfgs_run(start,n,beta,200.0,maxiter, deadline-20)
      c=compute_c5(y,n)
      if c<best:
         best=c; best_x=y.copy()
   return best_x,best
```
But if we perturb from best after some improvement, might lose. Only if remaining time. Keep simple no perturb? To escape, maybe include one perturbation at high beta. But if no improvement, perturb may produce new. Could do:

Let's detail `project_feasible` final:
```
def project_feasible(x, n):
    tgt = 0.5 * n
    y = np.clip(np.asarray(x, dtype=float), 0.0, 1.0)
    lo = float(y.min()) - 1.0
    hi = float(y.max()) + 1.0
    for _ in range(55):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        s = float(np.clip(y - mid, 0.0, 1.0).sum())
        if s > tgt:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid
    y = np.clip(y - 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0)
    for _ in range(8):
        diff = tgt - float(y.sum())
        if abs(diff) < 1e-13:
            break
        if diff > 0:
            room = 1.0 - y
            tot = float(room.sum())
            if tot <= 0.0:
                break
            y = np.clip(y + diff * room / tot, 0.0, 1.0)
        else:
            tot = float(y.sum())
            if tot <= 0.0:
                break
            y = np.clip(y + diff * y / tot, 0.0, 1.0)
    for _ in range(60):
        res = tgt - float(y.sum())
        if abs(res) <= 1e-14:
            break
        if res > 0:
            idx = np.where(y < 1.0)[0]
            if idx.size == 0:
                break
            add = min(res, 1.0 - float(y[idx[0]]))
            y[idx[0]] += add
        else:
            idx = np.where(y > 0.0)[0]
            if idx.size == 0:
                break
            dec = min(-res, float(y[idx[0]]))
            y[idx[0]] -= dec
    return y
```
Final loop might add to one element repeatedly; if res large, 60 not enough to add >60? Sum error from binary prop should tiny. Fine. Could use proportional again in final to be safe. We can improve final by distributing proportional if residual large before one-element:
The proportional loop already handles large residual first. The final residual maybe ~1e-13, one element enough. Good.

Potential if n=0? no.

`cdfeasible`:
```
def cdfeasible(M, lo, hi, unaff, cpos, apos, cneg, aneg, zmax, c0, a0):
    if M < unaff - 1e-11:
        return False, 0.0
    Lb = lo; Ub = hi
    if cpos.size:
        ub = float(np.min((M - cpos) / apos))
        if ub < Ub:
            Ub = ub
    if cneg.size:
        lb = float(np.max((M - cneg) / aneg))
        if lb > Lb:
            Lb = lb
    if zmax > M + 1e-11:
        return False, 0.0
    if Lb > Ub + 1e-10:
        return False, 0.0
    disc = a0 * a0 - 4.0 * (M - c0)
    if disc > 0.0:
        rt = float(np.sqrt(disc))
        r1 = 0.5 * (a0 - rt)
        r2 = 0.5 * (a0 + rt)
        tol = 1e-9
        if Lb > r1 + tol and Ub < r2 - tol:
            return False, 0.0
        if Ub <= r1 + tol:
            d = Ub
        elif Lb >= r2 - tol:
            d = Lb
        elif Lb < r1 + tol:
            d = Lb
        elif Ub > r2 - tol:
            d = Ub
        else:
            return False, 0.0
    else:
        d = 0.5 * (Lb + Ub)
    if d < lo: d = lo
    elif d > hi: d = hi
    return True, d
```
Tolerance 1e-9 may make solve stop slightly early. Final eval checks. But if M high by 1e-9 sum, C diff 3e-12, okay. Could use 1e-10. Use 1e-10. Binary 44 iterations.

`solve_cd_1d`:
```
def solve_cd_1d(c_aff, a, diag, unaff, lo, hi, current_max):
    c0 = c_aff[diag]
    a0 = a[diag]
    L = len(a)
    idx = np.arange(L)
    non = idx != diag
    pos_idx = np.where(non & (a > 1e-13))[0]
    neg_idx = np.where(non & (a < -1e-13))[0]
    zero_idx = np.where(non & (np.abs(a) <= 1e-13))[0]
    cpos = c_aff[pos_idx]; apos = a[pos_idx]
    cneg = c_aff[neg_idx]; aneg = a[neg_idx]
    zmax = float(c_aff[zero_idx].max()) if zero_idx.size else -np.inf
    fend = eval_cd_points(c_aff,a,diag,unaff,[lo,hi])
    high = min(float(current_max), float(np.min(fend))) + 1e-8
    if high < 1e-8: high = 1e-8
    low = 0.0
    for _ in range(44):
        mid = 0.5*(low+high)
        ok,_ = cdfeasible(mid, lo, hi, unaff, cpos,apos,cneg,aneg,zmax,c0,a0)
        if ok:
            high = mid
        else:
            low = mid
    ok,d = cdfeasible(high+1e-8, lo, hi, unaff, cpos,apos,cneg,aneg,zmax,c0,a0)
    if not ok:
       d=0.0
    cand_ds = [d, lo, hi, 0.0]
    vals = eval_cd_points(c_aff,a,diag,unaff,cand_ds)
    imin = int(np.argmin(vals))
    fd = float(vals[imin])
    if fd < float(current_max) - 1e-9:
        return float(cand_ds[imin])
    return None
```
Could high be less than true minimum if endpoint fenders use approximate eval? fenders exact. Good. But if high = min endpoint maybe lower than line constraints? It's actual max at endpoint so feasible. Good.
If current_max is already lower than endpoint (no improvement), high=current+1e-8. Good.

Now, `coord_descent`:
```
def coord_descent(h,n,deadline,max_passes=100,proj_every=30,stall_limit=4):
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    best_c=compute_c5(h,n); best_h=h.copy()
    if max_passes <=0: return best_h,best_c
    pre=precompute_cd(n)
    rng=np.random.RandomState(12345)
    stall=0
    for pass_no in range(max_passes):
       if time.time() > deadline: break
       c=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full')
       start_max=float(c.max())
       seen_min=start_max
       passed=False
       order=rng.permutation(n)
       for cnt,p in enumerate(order):
          if time.time() > deadline: break
          if cnt and cnt % proj_every == 0:
             h=project_feasible(h,n)
             c=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full')
             start_max=float(c.max())
             seen_min=start_max
          mlo,...=pre[p]
          a=np.zeros(2*R+1)
          ...
          c_aff=c[mlo:mhi+1]
          unaff=...
          curmax=float(c.max())
          newd=solve_cd_1d(c_aff,a,R,unaff,-h[p],1-h[p],curmax)
          if newd is not None:
             h[p]+=newd
             delta=a*newd; delta[R]-=newd*newd
             c[mlo:mhi+1]+=delta
             passed=True
             cm=float(c.max())
             if cm < seen_min: seen_min=cm
       if time.time() > deadline: break
       h=project_feasible(h,n)
       end_c=compute_c5(h,n)
       if end_c < best_c - 1e-12:
          best_c=end_c; best_h=h.copy(); stall=0
       else:
          if seen_min < start_max - 1e-7:
             # infeasible progress, continue
             continue
          stall += 1
          if stall >= stall_limit:
             break
    final_c=compute_c5(h,n)
    if final_c < best_c:
       best_c=final_c; best_h=h.copy()
    return best_h,best_c
```

def lbfgs_polish(x,n,deadline):
    x=project_feasible(x,n); best=compute_c5(x,n); best_x=x.copy()
    stages = ((50.0,40,20.0),(200.0,60,20.0),(1000.0,80,50.0),(5000.0,100,100.0),(20000.0,140,200.0))
    for beta,mi,mu in stages:
       if time.time() > deadline - 40: break
       y=lbfgs_run(best_x,n,beta,mu,mi,deadline-25)
       c=compute_c5(y,n)
       if c < best:
           best=c; best_x=y
    return best_x,best
```

```python
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize
import time

LB_STOP = [1e100]


def compute_c5(h):
    n = len(h)
    dx = 2.0 / n
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")) * dx)


def project_feasible(x, n):
    tgt = 0.5 * n
    y = np.clip(np.asarray(x, dtype=float), 0.0, 1.0)
    lo = float(y.min()) - 1.0
    hi = float(y.max()) + 1.0
    for _ in range(55):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        s = float(np.clip(y - mid, 0.0, 1.0).sum())
        if s > tgt:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid
    y = np.clip(y - 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0)
    for _ in range(8):
        diff = tgt - float(y.sum())
        if abs(diff) < 1e-13:
            break
        if diff > 0:
            room = 1.0 - y
            tot = float(room.sum())
            if tot <= 0.0:
                break
            y = np.clip(y + diff * room / tot, 0.0, 1.0)
        else:
            tot = float(y.sum())
            if tot <= 0.0:
                break
            y = np.clip(y + diff * y / tot, 0.0, 1.0)
    for _ in range(60):
        res = tgt - float(y.sum())
        if abs(res) <= 1e-14:
            break
        if res > 0:
            idx = np.where(y < 1.0)[0]
            if idx.size == 0:
                break
            add = min(res, 1.0 - float(y[idx[0]]))
            y[idx[0]] += add
        else:
            idx = np.where(y > 0.0)[0]
            if idx.size == 0:
                break
            dec = min(-res, float(y[idx[0]]))
            y[idx[0]] -= dec
    return y


def eval_cd_points(c_aff, a, diag, unaff, ds):
    ds = np.asarray(ds, dtype=float)
    vals = c_aff[:, None] + a[:, None] * ds[None, :]
    vals[diag, :] -= ds * ds
    f = vals.max(axis=0)
    if unaff > 0.0:
        np.maximum(f, unaff, out=f)
    return f


def cdfeasible(M, lo, hi, unaff, cpos, apos, cneg, aneg, zmax, c0, a0):
    if M < unaff - 1e-11:
        return False, 0.0
    Lb = lo
    Ub = hi
    if cpos.size:
        ub = float(np.min((M - cpos) / apos))
        if ub < Ub:
            Ub = ub
    if cneg.size:
        lb = float(np.max((M - cneg) / aneg))
        if lb > Lb:
            Lb = lb
    if zmax > M + 1e-11:
        return False, 0.0
    if Lb > Ub + 1e-10:
        return False, 0.0
    disc = a0 * a0 - 4.0 * (M - c0)
    if disc > 0.0:
        rt = float(np.sqrt(disc))
        r1 = 0.5 * (a0 - rt)
        r2 = 0.5 * (a0 + rt)
        tol = 1e-10
        if Lb > r1 + tol and Ub < r2 - tol:
            return False, 0.0
        if Ub <= r1 + tol:
            d = Ub
        elif Lb >= r2 - tol:
            d = Lb
        elif Lb < r1 + tol:
            d = Lb
        elif Ub > r2 - tol:
            d = Ub
        else:
            return False, 0.0
    else:
        d = 0.5 * (Lb + Ub)
    if d < lo:
        d = lo
    elif d > hi:
        d = hi
    return True, float(d)


def solve_cd_1d(c_aff, a, diag, unaff, lo, hi, current_max):
    c0 = float(c_aff[diag])
    a0 = float(a[diag])
    pos_idx = np.flatnonzero(a > 1e-13)
    pos_idx = pos_idx[pos_idx != diag]
    neg_idx = np.flatnonzero(a < -1e-13)
    neg_idx = neg_idx[neg_idx != diag]
    zero_idx = np.flatnonzero(np.abs(a) <= 1e-13)
    zero_idx = zero_idx[zero_idx != diag]
    cpos = c_aff[pos_idx]
    apos = a[pos_idx]
    cneg = c_aff[neg_idx]
    aneg = a[neg_idx]
    zmax = float(c_aff[zero_idx].max()) if zero_idx.size else -np.inf

    fend = eval_cd_points(c_aff, a, diag, unaff, [lo, hi])
    high = min(float(current_max), float(np.min(fend))) + 1e-8
    if high < 1e-8:
        high = 1e-8
    low = 0.0
    for _ in range(40):
        mid = 0.5 * (low + high)
        ok, _ = cdfeasible(mid, lo, hi, unaff, cpos, apos, cneg, aneg, zmax, c0, a0)
        if ok:
            high = mid
        else:
            low = mid
    ok, d = cdfeasible(high + 1e-8, lo, hi, unaff, cpos, apos, cneg, aneg, zmax, c0, a0)
    if not ok:
        d = 0.0
    cand = [d, lo, hi, 0.0]
    vals = eval_cd_points(c_aff, a, diag, unaff, cand)
    imin = int(np.argmin(vals))
    if float(vals[imin]) < float(current_max) - 1e-9:
        return float(cand[imin])
    return None


def precompute_cd(n):
    pre = []
    empty = np.empty(0, dtype=np.int64)
    for p in range(n):
        R = max(p, n - 1 - p)
        mlo = n - 1 - R
        mhi = n - 1 + R
        k = np.arange(-R, R + 1, dtype=np.int64)
        vf = (p - k >= 0) & (p - k < n)
        vs = (p + k >= 0) & (p + k < n)
        posf = np.nonzero(vf)[0]
        idxf = (p - k[vf]).astype(np.int64) if posf.size else empty
        poss = np.nonzero(vs)[0]
        idxs = (p + k[vs]).astype(np.int64) if poss.size else empty
        pre.append((mlo, mhi, posf, idxf, poss, idxs, R))
    return pre


def coord_descent(h, n, deadline, max_passes=120, proj_every=30, stall_limit=4):
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    best_c = compute_c5(h, n)
    best_h = h.copy()
    if max_passes <= 0:
        return best_h, best_c
    pre = precompute_cd(n)
    rng = np.random.RandomState(12345)
    stall = 0
    for _ in range(max_passes):
        if time.time() > deadline:
            break
        c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
        start_max = float(c.max())
        seen_min = start_max
        order = rng.permutation(n)
        for cnt, p in enumerate(order):
            if time.time() > deadline:
                break
            if cnt and cnt % proj_every == 0:
                h = project_feasible(h, n)
                c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
                start_max = float(c.max())
                seen_min = start_max
            h0 = float(h[p])
            if h0 <= 0.0:
                lo = 0.0
                hi = 1.0
            elif h0 >= 1.0:
                lo = -1.0
                hi = 0.0
            else:
                lo = -h0
                hi = 1.0 - h0
            if hi - lo < 1e-13:
                continue
            mlo, mhi, posf, idxf, poss, idxs, R = pre[p]
            a = np.zeros(2 * R + 1, dtype=float)
            if posf.size:
                a[posf] += 1.0 - h[idxf]
            if poss.size:
                a[poss] -= h[idxs]
            c_aff = c[mlo:mhi + 1]
            if mlo > 0:
                unaff = float(c[:mlo].max())
            else:
                unaff = 0.0
            if mhi + 1 < c.size:
                tmp = float(c[mhi + 1:].max())
                if tmp > unaff:
                    unaff = tmp
            curmax = float(c.max())
            if unaff >= curmax - 1e-10:
                continue
            newd = solve_cd_1d(c_aff, a, R, unaff, lo, hi, curmax)
            if newd is not None:
                h[p] = h0 + newd
                h[p] = float(np.clip(h[p], 0.0, 1.0))
                delta = a * newd
                delta[R] -= newd * newd
                c[mlo:mhi + 1] += delta
                cm = float(c.max())
                if cm < seen_min:
                    seen_min = cm
        if time.time() > deadline:
            break
        h = project_feasible(h, n)
        end_c = compute_c5(h, n)
        if end_c < best_c - 1e-12:
            best_c = end_c
            best_h = h.copy()
            stall = 0
        else:
            if seen_min < start_max - 1e-7:
                continue
            stall += 1
            if stall >= stall_limit:
                break
    final_c = compute_c5(h, n)
    if final_c < best_c:
        best_c = final_c
        best_h = h.copy()
    return best_h, best_c


def smooth_value(x, n, beta, mu):
    dx = 2.0 / n
    c = np.correlate(x, 1.0 - x, mode="full")
    m = float(c.max())
    z = beta * (c - m)
    z = np.clip(z, -745.0, 0.0)
    ez = np.exp(z)
    logsum = float(np.log(ez.sum()))
    base = dx * (m + logsum / beta)
    err = (float(x.sum()) - 0.5 * n) * dx
    return base + mu * err * err


def smooth_grad(x, n, beta, mu):
    dx = 2.0 / n
    c = np.correlate(x, 1.0 - x, mode="full")
    m = float(c.max())
    z = beta * (c - m)
    z = np.clip(z, -745.0, 0.0)
    ez = np.exp(z)
    q = ez / float(ez.sum())
    b = 1.0 - x
    F = np.zeros(n)
    G = np.zeros(n)
    q0 = q[n - 1]
    F += q0 * b
    G += q0 * x
    for t in range(1, n):
        rem = n - t
        qp = q[n - 1 + t]
        if qp != 0.0:
            F[t:] += qp * b[:rem]
            G[:rem] += qp * x[t:]
        qm = q[n - 1 - t]
        if qm != 0.0:
            F[:rem] += qm * b[t:]
            G[t:] += qm * x[:rem]
    grad = (F - G) * dx
    if mu:
        err = (float(x.sum()) - 0.5 * n) * dx
        grad += 2.0 * mu * err * dx
    return grad


def _lbfun(x, n, beta, mu):
    return smooth_value(x, n, beta, mu)


def _lbjac(x, n, beta, mu):
    return smooth_grad(x, n, beta, mu)


def lb_callback(xk):
    return time.time() > LB_STOP[0]


def lbfgs_run(x0, n, beta, mu, maxiter, stop_at):
    LB_STOP[0] = stop_at
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    try:
        res = minimize(
            _lbfun,
            x0,
            args=(n, beta, mu),
            jac=_lbjac,
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            bounds=bounds,
            callback=lb_callback,
            options={"maxiter": int(maxiter), "ftol": 1e-12, "gtol": 1e-7, "maxls": 25},
        )
        if res.x is None:
            return x0
        return project_feasible(np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float), n)
    except Exception:
        return x0


def lbfgs_polish(x, n, deadline):
    x = project_feasible(x, n)
    best_c = compute_c5(x, n)
    best_h = x.copy()
    stages = (
        (50.0, 40, 20.0),
        (200.0, 60, 20.0),
        (1000.0, 80, 50.0),
        (5000.0, 100, 100.0),
        (20000.0, 130, 200.0),
    )
    for beta, maxiter, mu in stages:
        if time.time() > deadline - 40:
            break
        y = lbfgs_run(best_h, n, beta, mu, maxiter, deadline - 25)
        c = compute_c5(y, n)
        if c < best_c:
            best_c = c
            best_h = y.copy()
    return best_h, best_c


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    B = float(budget_s)
    if B <= 20.0:
        try:
            h = np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
        except Exception:
            h = np.full(300, 0.5)
        n = len(h)
        h = project_feasible(h, n)
        return h, compute_c5(h, n), n

    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    try:
        h = np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
        n = int(len(h))
    except Exception:
        n = 700
        x = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n, endpoint=False)
        h = 0.5 + 0.35 * np.sin(np.pi * x) + 0.15 * np.sin(3.0 * np.pi * x)
        h += 0.04 * rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, n)

    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = compute_c5(h, n)

    cd1_dead = t0 + max(20.0, 0.35 * B)
    lb_dead = t0 + max(cd1_dead - t0 + 35.0, 0.70 * B)
    final_dead = t0 + B - 5.0

    h, c = coord_descent(h, n, cd1_dead, max_passes=120, proj_every=30, stall_limit=4)
    if c < best_c:
        best_c = c
        best_h = h.copy()

    h, c = lbfgs_polish(h, n, lb_dead)
    if c < best_c:
        best_c = c
        best_h = h.copy()

    h, c = coord_descent(h, n, final_dead, max_passes=2000, proj_every=30, stall_limit=6)
    if c < best_c:
        best_c = c
        best_h = h.copy()

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, len(best_h))
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h, len(best_h))
    return best_h, best_c, int(len(best_h))
```
